# B1 Vocabulary List (Wortschatz)

A curated collection of authentic, level-appropriate German vocabulary for the **B1 (Intermediate / Mittelstufe)** level according to the CEFR standards. Each entry includes the word with grammatical markers, English definition, topic category, a contextual example sentence with English translation, and direct reference links.

[B1 Grammatik](./Grammatik.md) | [Verbs](./Verbs.md) | [Adjectives](./Adjectives.md) | [Cases](./Cases.md) | [Prepositions](./Prepositions.md) | [Pronouns](./Pronouns.md)

---

| German Word | English Translation | Topic Category | Example Sentence | Dictionary & Conjugation Links |
